              - separator [ref=e627]
              - heading "Sobre este artículo" [level=1] [ref=e628]
              - list [ref=e629]:
                - listitem [ref=e630]: 【Diseño cómodo y fácil de embalar】Los auriculares están hechos de piel sintética sintética, impermeable y no se deforma fácilmente. La almohadilla suave para las orejas va alrededor de tu oreja para garantizar un ajuste cómodo. Además, evita que los auriculares Bluetooth sobre la oreja resuenen entre la carcasa de los auriculares y los huesos de los oídos cuando el volumen es demasiado alto, protegiendo tus oídos con un buen cojín. La diadema es ajustable, elástica para adaptarse a una variedad de formas de cabeza.

                        - link "Best headphones I’ve ever had" [ref=e2018] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - /url: /-/es/gp/customer-reviews/R1ZK7MNV5FDTMA/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_ttl?_encoding=UTF8&ie=UTF8
                          - heading "Best headphones I’ve ever had" [level=5] [ref=e2019]

                        - generic [ref=e2021]:
                          - 'link "Color: Oro rosa" [ref=e2022] [cursor=pointer]':
                            - /url: /-/es/product-reviews/B0BMLXSNG8/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format
                          - link "Compra Verificada" [ref=e2025] [cursor=pointer]:
                            - /url: /-/es/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?_encoding=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA
                        - generic [ref=e2028]:
                          - generic [ref=e2034]:
                            - paragraph [ref=e2035]: These are absolutely the best headphones I’ve ever had. I bought these Feb 23, 2026 and I just NOW today, April 3, 2026 am having to charge them for the first time since I opened them. That’s insane, I would’ve had to charge the 🍎 ear buds 10 times over in this amount of time.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2036]: They came most charged straight out the box, though I did put them on the charger for about and hour and they were fully charged, and only TODAY did they say “battery low” AMAZING.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2037]: I am a music connoisseur, I am always listening to music or podcasts while cleaning my house, doing dishes, laundry, anything and these have stayed charged the entire time.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2038]: I love the color, very beautiful. I love how compact they are. They are very durable. The microphone is great too, people can hear me clearly on calls when I need to be hands free, during FaceTimes or when I’m multitasking. My phone also picks up the audio amazingly through the microphone so I can still use Siri and talk to text as well. The noise cancellation is there for the most part, I’d give it like an 8/10, though I have a small child and don’t necessarily want insanely noise cancellation, because then chaos would break loose lol but it’s still there and it cancels out a lot
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2039]: The cord is a run of the mill cord, I don’t mind that it is a shorter one because I don’t use them while they are charging, whole reason I bought cordless headphones in the first place lol
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2040]: The ear pads are super comfortable, fit perfectly around my ears, the how much you can adjust them when you need to. As well as the pad that goes over your head, very comfortable!
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2041]: Overall worth the money, I did buy these when they were on sale, but I would still buy them full price over and over again because they are just that good.

                          - /url: /-/es/gp/customer-reviews/R36SKBLSH9O5JA/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_ttl?_encoding=UTF8&ie=UTF8
                          - heading "Excellent!" [level=5] [ref=e2079]
                        - generic [ref=e2080]: Calificado en Estados Unidos el 10 de marzo de 2026
                        - generic [ref=e2081]:
                          - 'link "Color: Azul" [ref=e2082] [cursor=pointer]':
                            - /url: /-/es/product-reviews/B0CC3LN5YN/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format
                          - link "Compra Verificada" [ref=e2085] [cursor=pointer]:
                            - /url: /-/es/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?_encoding=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA
                        - paragraph [ref=e2095]: This thing is terrific. I originally bought it for using with my iPad, and for using when on the treadmill. It works great for both. The bass is terrific quality, the sound can go way loud, I can’t go full volume it’s powerful. I used it to jog outside today because it was nice day. I jogged around my backyard which is a pretty big area of almost a 1/4 mile when I complete the circle, like a track. I left my phone on the deck and the WiFi had no problems reaching it and I had no sound quality drop the entire time. It is also easy to use, you can skip the song or adjust volume right on the headset. So the WiFi range is really nice, I won’t have to carry my phone when I jog outside. I also have no problems with it when jog bouncing or sliding, they stay on secure and comfortable. I prefer headphones over ear plugs, and I would recommend these headphones for exercise or with whatever device you use for movies, videos, or books. The sound quality and noise cancelling is very good quality and value for the money.

                        - generic [ref=e2180]: Calificado en Estados Unidos el 12 de abril de 2026

                        - generic [ref=e2188]:
                          - generic [ref=e2194]:
                            - paragraph [ref=e2195]: These headphones look like the higher end brand name ones. The pink color is fantastic. They fold up nicely and the construction of them is nice.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2196]: They are designed with comfort in mind. They cushion sits against the outside of your ear but does not actually touch your ear. It is like a nice little cocoon for your ears. So cozy. And they are adjustable to fit a variety of head sizes.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2197]: They sound terrific. They are clear and crisp and have a good amount of volume. I am able to hear some of what is happening around me at lower volumes so I do not know if I would say they are true noise canceling but I can shut out my coworkers when I turn them up.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2198]: They connect very easily to wifi. My phone found them immediately. It was simple set up.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2199]: I have had them just over a few weeks and use them nearly every day for a good hour. I have not had to recharge them yet. So the battery life seems to be great.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2200]: My only negative is that sometimes when I go to turn them off, I have to push the button over and over to get the lady in my head to say "power off" Then I try to answer my phone and I forget I am still on my headphones.

                        - link "Jennifer" [ref=e2372] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - /url: /-/es/gp/profile/amzn1.account.AEWAVBTVXQNL2GLJQHJONMUYKKLQ
                          - img [ref=e2375]
                          - generic [ref=e2377]: Jennifer
                        - generic [ref=e2380]: 5 de 5 estrellas
                        - link "Works great" [ref=e2381] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - /url: /-/es/gp/customer-reviews/R142NNSKEBEWC7/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_ttl?_encoding=UTF8&ie=UTF8
                          - heading "Works great" [level=5] [ref=e2382]
                        - generic [ref=e2383]: Calificado en Estados Unidos el 24 de diciembre de 2023
                        - generic [ref=e2384]:
                          - 'link "Color: Oro rosa" [ref=e2385] [cursor=pointer]':
                            - /url: /-/es/product-reviews/B0BMLXSNG8/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format
                          - link "Compra Verificada" [ref=e2388] [cursor=pointer]:
                            - /url: /-/es/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?_encoding=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA
                        - generic [ref=e2391]:
                          - generic [ref=e2397]:
                            - paragraph [ref=e2398]: I've been using it just over a month now.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2399]: "Connectivity/Sound:"
                            - paragraph [ref=e2400]: ~My main purpose in buying these was the multipoint connectivity. I need to quickly switch between my phone and computer, and these make it very convenient since it can connect to two devices at the same time. If you start playing media on one device and then start playing media on a second device, playback on device 1 will automatically pause while device 2 plays. My only gripe here is that if you turn the headphones off and back on, it only auto-connects to one device, and you need to manually connect to the 2nd device again. It would be nice if it could reconnect to both devices when powered on so I'm ready to go. Overall, I'm really happy with this feature.
                            - paragraph [ref=e2401]: ~The range is really good; I've walked out to my car forgetting that I have them on and it surprisingly remained connected. None of my other headsets reach that far.
                            - paragraph [ref=e2402]: ~I'm no audio engineer, but the sound quality is great. There is a function to change the equalizer settings if you want. As you press the button, robot voice says where in the cycle you are.
                            - paragraph [ref=e2403]: ~The set comes with an 18 inch charging cable and a 47 inch 3.5mm audio cable. Of course, you can use the audio cable while the headphones are powered off. There is no noticeable difference in the sound quality vs using Bluetooth.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2404]: "Battery:"
                            - paragraph [ref=e2405]: This is definitely the 2nd best part about these. It is charged out of the box, but I charged it ~10 min to get it to full. They last FOREVER. I started using them on 11/21 at 10:45am on a full charge and used it nearly all day, every day after. I didn't get a "battery low" warning until 11/28 at 2:39pm. That's some serious juice. On that note, the only con is that said "battery low" warning does NOT mean your battery is that low. I wanted to see just how long I'd have before it died. That dayum robot continued to warn me every 3 minutes (literally--I tracked it) until after 1am the next day when I just couldn't take it anymore and needed to go to bed. Almost 12 hrs of "battery low"....really? I suppose it's going off some percentage of battery remaining, but it should have been set to a much more reasonable threshold, like 1-2 hours remaining (considering the very long battery life, as most devices would shut off within like 20 min of the warning). Every time it said the warning, my microphone/audit would cut out as I couldn't hear people on video calls and they couldn't hear me. This is a long time to deal with that. Thank you for reading my rant.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2406]: "Fit:"
                            - paragraph [ref=e2407]: I wear them for several hours a day, sometimes 10+, and have no complaints about comfort. If you wear them while doing heavy physical activity, they do trap heat, just a heads up.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2408]: "Misc.:"
                            - paragraph [ref=e2409]: ~It takes unusually long to power off, enough time to make me wonder if something is wrong. Not a problem, just seems unintuitive to have to hold the power button for like 6 seconds (seriously, count that out and see how awkward it is just to shut a device off). It powers on more quickly.
                            - paragraph [ref=e2410]: ~I'm grateful that there is no obnoxiously loud chiming with the controls. Quick beep or robot voice, nothing crazy.
                            - paragraph [ref=e2411]: ~Press +/- button once to go to next/prev track. Press & hold for volume up/down. If anyone was wondering.
                            - paragraph [ref=e2412]: ~I need a mute function for these conference calls where I walk away until I'm needed. Mfr dropped the ball on that one.
                            - paragraph
                            - paragraph [ref=e2413]: "Conclusion:"
                            - paragraph [ref=e2414]: For about ~$16 on sale, these are absolutely worth the purchase. I bought a backup for if/when these ultimately break down as anything cheap will do eventually (some other reviews confirm this). You will need to get a case separately. Until this starts giving me problems, I'd recommend it.

                        - paragraph [ref=e2479]: Cómodo, buen sonido. La lluvia muy ligera veo no lo estropea, resistente, funciona para deporte exterior. Fácil de conectar hasta dos dispositivos al mismo tiempo y se auto-regula fácilmente porque toma la señal del último dispositivo, ejemplo está conectado al portátil y si pones play al youtube del movil empieza a sonar de ahí. Tiene buen ver. Relación precio calidad buena. comprado 13 junio 2024 y hoy 23 noviembre 2024 comunico reseña confirmando que funciona plenamente. había mirado mucho antes de comprar, de momento satisfecho.
                        - button "Revisión del informe" [ref=e2483] [cursor=pointer]: Informe
                    - listitem [ref=e2484]:

                        - generic [ref=e2497]: 5 de 5 estrellas
                        - heading "Guclu, Doygun Seslere sahip; Kullanisli Bir Kulaklik." [level=5] [ref=e2498]
                        - generic [ref=e2499]: Calificado en Turquía el 31 de marzo de 2025
                        - generic [ref=e2500]:
                          - 'link "Color: Negro" [ref=e2501] [cursor=pointer]':
                            - /url: /-/es/product-reviews/B0BRQ7PZG4/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format
                          - link "Compra Verificada" [ref=e2504] [cursor=pointer]:
                            - /url: /-/es/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?_encoding=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA
                        - generic [ref=e2513]:
                          - paragraph [ref=e2514]: Ses frekan araliklari cok guclu, dusuk frekanslara tepkisi cok iyi. Volume, mukemmel.
                          - paragraph [ref=e2515]: Bir derdi var, Orta tonlara kayan baslari abartiyor. Equilaser'siz kullanmak istemeyebiliyorsun (ki, doygun tonlari severim ama 200-400Hz arasini saliveriyor insanin kulagina; pek saglikli ve keyifli degil). Dinlettiginiz cihazda bir equilaser uygulamasi ile bu frekanslari kisarsaniz, tadina doyum olmuyor.
                          - paragraph
                          - paragraph [ref=e2516]: "Emin olamadigim icin kulakligi ters takarak (sol, saga) da denedigim bir ufak derdi var gibi: Sol arafin sesi biraz daha az geliyor ve tum frekanslarda degil. Garip bir his yaratabiliyor. Her zaman olmuyor ve cok dikkat etmedim ama sanki iki cihaza birden baglandiginda bu sorun ortadan kalkiyor."
                          - paragraph
                          - paragraph [ref=e2517]: Kendi uzerinde bulunan Eq ozelligi bence asiri gereksiz. Keske seceneklerden birinde "No-Equilaser" olsaydi.
                          - paragraph
                          - paragraph [ref=e2518]: Bataryasini bugune dek bitiremedim, denk gelmedi. Belki 4-5 gun sarj etmedigim olmustur.
                          - paragraph
                          - paragraph [ref=e2519]: Ozetle, hicbir halukarda pisman degilim, halen her disari ciktigimda kulaklarimda degilse cantamdadir ve 'equilaser'in dogru yazimina bakmaya usendigim icin ozur dilerim.

                        - generic [ref=e2632]:
                          - paragraph [ref=e2633]: Was finding it really hard to get some sports buds at a reasonable price, and ended up getting a few different pairs of wireless in-ear. Sound was ok, as long as you got position right, and comfort was ok, as long as you got position right. Plus one bud always ran out way quicker than the other. So started looking at the old style over ear type for using in and around house and garden.
                          - paragraph [ref=e2634]: Considered these alongside a couple of others. The features for the price was the clinch, but I was still a bit skeptical about the performance. Was happily proved wrong; really good sound quality, battery life is brilliant - they get used pretty much daily for a couple of hours and only need charging every 4-5 weeks, even when I forget to turn them off. Connecting with two devices simultaneously is great - the only drawback being that it’s easy to inadvertently switch to the second device when you don’t really mean to. Comfort wise, they are good for a few hours and I often leave them on when I’m in the middle of something and the sound has stopped for whatever reason (device shutdown, end of podcast or playlist, that kind of thing) without registering fully and removing them.
                          - paragraph [ref=e2635]: So happy with them overall that planning on getting a second pair to leave upstairs.
